+# S4-02d — analytic and Finding worker schedule
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: false, provided this remains an operations/read-contract slice with no new owner-facing copy, panel, or visual state. If implementation changes any of those, reopen the flag and obtain the owner checkpoint. Fresh independent implementation review remains required.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials, prerequisite of S5-01
+Depends on: registry `requires` `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work` (resolved). `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` is a separate S5-01 prerequisite, not an input to this schedule.
+Branch when activated: `task/S4-02d-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-14
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+An environment-owned, bounded tick runs S3 `process-jobs`, then S4 `match-findings` for every active tenant/Site, then the existing S4-02c consequence drain. A late accepted reading reaches a revised FindingRevision and consequence without an operator invoking the two Leg B commands. Operators can see per-Site backlog, last success/failure, retry and stopped-worker conditions. S5-01 cannot mark a selected window CURRENT while relevant upstream work or an unobserved/stopped worker could leave it behind.
+
+Authority: `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md` R1, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`. This packet owns registry node `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule` (S). The separate F1 node owns run-1 sequence numbering and money limitation semantics; this packet does not change S3 cohort rules, Finding limits or price eligibility.
+
+Built anchors: `.ai/CODE_STATE.md` S3-01/S4-01/S4-02c; `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assess_dispatch.py` (`process-jobs`, `match-findings --tenant-id --site-id`); `analytics/assessment_worker.py` (reconcile, leased jobs and retry); `backend/assetops_backend/recompute_consequences.py` and `financials/recompute.py` (Site claim, attempt-derived retry); `backend/README-db.md` (one-minute external-timer runbook); `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` (Leg A/B proof). The current `process-jobs` CLI reconciles all assessed Sites and drains up to 1000 jobs; `match-findings` is a one-shot Site matcher; the consequence CLI serves only the configured development tenant and one named Site. Do not claim those CLIs already cover all active production Sites.
+
+## Schedule, state and S5 contract
+
+1. **Owned Site inventory and cadence.** Configure one deployment-owned timer (Windows Task Scheduler or systemd) at a documented cadence, initially one minute. A server-side orchestrator enumerates active imported tenant/Sites from the authoritative persisted Site registry on every tick, including newly activated Sites; use stable tenant/Site IDs, never a hard-coded demo list or gateway-supplied scope. Define and test the exact active predicate and exclusion of inactive/foreign Sites. Bound Sites per tick, S3 jobs per tick and consequence items per Site, with a durable cursor or fair ordering so a busy Site cannot starve another. The existing S3 worker's global reconcile must still run; expose a bounded drain limit rather than relying on the CLI's default 1000. S4 matching runs only after S3 work relevant to that Site has reached a completed basis; if S3 remains due, leased, failed or deferred, record it as pending and defer that Site's S4/currentness promotion. Match the Site even when no new S3 job exists if a selected assessment/configuration/model basis became dirty. Then drain that Site's consequence work after Finding commits. One tick may leave work pending; later ticks resume it.
+2. **Concurrency and recovery.** Use a database claim for the schedule/tick and per-Site processing, or prove an equivalent cross-host exclusion; a local timer's “do not start another instance” is insufficient. Preserve S3's lease/retry and S4-02c's Site claim/append idempotency. A crash releases or expires the claim, and the next tick resumes without duplicate assessment, FindingRevision or consequence basis. Bound wall time per tick and per Site, including a hung `match-findings`/database call; report timeout as failure and leave work retryable. Use a documented retry/backoff for schedule-level failures, including failed Site discovery, S3 worker exceptions and matcher exceptions; do not silently advance success. Do not add a second independent retry mechanism to S3 or financial work rows.
+3. **Observable durable state.** Persist or derive a tenant/Site-scoped schedule record with last tick start, last success, last failure (stage, typed code and time), running/claim age, retry due, selected/processed/deferred counts and remaining S3/S4/consequence backlog. Define S4 backlog by a durable dirty basis/watermark or equivalent exact comparison between selected assessment revisions and matched Finding basis, not by counting only queued S3 jobs. Expose a read-only operator report/CLI with Site identity and an aggregate; distinguish `RUNNING`, `BACKLOG`, `RETRY_DUE`, `FAILED`, `STOPPED` (missed heartbeat beyond a declared threshold), and healthy idle. A successful zero-work tick advances the heartbeat. Failed and repeatedly skipped/hung ticks cannot look healthy. Carry S4-02c review I1/I2: show permanently failing consequence attempt count/age and growing backlog without capping or deleting attempts; make repeated Site-claim skips and stale in-flight claims an alarm with a concrete recovery procedure. Document the timer identity, credentials, cadence, timeouts, alert threshold, restart and commands in `backend/README-db.md`.
+4. **S5-01 consumption.** Provide a scoped status/watermark read that S5-01 can join to its exact selected half-open Site window: accepted-source watermark, selected S3 run/revision, S4 matched revision/basis and outstanding/failed consequence work plus schedule heartbeat. A first build with outstanding work is `PENDING`; an older exact result with an advanced source/analytic basis or dirty work is `STALE_PROJECTION`; a failed worker with no usable prior result is `FAILED`, with the exact upstream stage/reason available. A stopped or unobserved schedule cannot certify CURRENT, even when a queue query is empty. Only a healthy observed pass after the accepted-source watermark, completed relevant S3 work, matched S4 basis and completed relevant consequence work may permit CURRENT; S5-01 still applies its separate F1 money rule and `CURRENT/PRICED` same-basis check. Keep old IDs and lineage readable. This packet exposes the contract and tests its gate; S5-01 implements the Overview read/UI.
+
+## Phases and focused proof
+
+Each phase is a reviewable result fitting one Claude Implementer context below approximately 120k tokens; obtain an independent fresh review before proceeding.
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Bounded scheduled orchestration | Persisted active-Site enumeration, fair bounded tick, ordered S3 → S4 → consequence invocation, cross-host claim, timeout/restart behavior and installable timer configuration. Prove two tenants, newly activated Site, inactive/foreign Site refusal, more Sites/jobs than one tick limit, two concurrent timers, crash/restart, idempotent replay and no S4 promotion ahead of due/leased/failed S3 work. |
+| 2. Health and currentness handoff | Durable/derived per-Site stage and backlog report, last success/failure, retry due and stopped/hung alarm; selected-window S5 status/watermark seam. Prove idle heartbeat, failing matcher, failed S3 job, repeated claim skips, timer stopped beyond threshold, permanent consequence failure and recovery. Assert pending/stale/failed versus eligible CURRENT after reload; no queue-empty shortcut. |
+| 3. Automatic late-power walkthrough | Extend or reuse `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` in an isolated database. Retain Leg A exact accepted ID, stale queued work, same-revision `POWER_NOT_IN_ASSESSMENT_INPUTS` and history. Release the withheld GOOD mean, then drive only the configured schedule/ticks (no manual `process-jobs` or `match-findings` Leg B calls). Assert the revised FindingRevision cites the late ID; its consequence work reaches the new CURRENT result and passes the bounded S5 eligibility gate only after all upstream work is complete. Record actual run/revision/work/attempt/consequence IDs, watermarks, backlog and heartbeat before/after, replay and stopped-worker state. If run-1 admission numbering triggers F1 ambiguity, assert no S5 amount and use the existing source-ordered variant for the positive amount path; do not fix F1 here. |
+
+Reviewer checks Site inventory and isolation, per-tick limits/fairness, ordering, claim/timeout/retry, restart idempotency, stage-specific errors, heartbeat and backlog truth, currentness under race, and the no-manual-Leg-B artifact. Run focused Postgres worker tests and the isolated walkthrough plus applicable architecture/workflow guards. `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` stays false only while the output remains operator/read-contract work; S5-01 retains the owner visual checkpoint.
+
+## Risks and carries
+
+- S3's existing global `reconcile_all()` can be expensive as Sites grow; measure a multi-Site tick and keep bounded scheduling truthful. Do not omit reconcile to meet a timer budget.
+- S4 matching currently scans a Site's selected reads. Large Sites may require a reviewed bounded cursor or dirty-window selection to meet the tick limit; do not mark a partially matched Site caught up.
+- S4-02c retries have no terminal cap: a deterministic failed row can append about 90 attempts/day. Keep the growing count and backlog observable and alertable; do not hide it by reporting only due work.
+- A hung consequence runner can hold a Site claim while later ticks report skipped. Treat repeated skips/claim age as a stopped-progress alarm, with operator recovery; do not count skipped as success.
+- Backdated configuration/model changes can dirty assessed windows without a fresh evidence job. The S3 reconcile and S4 basis comparison must detect them before CURRENT is possible.
+
+Registry comparison: `queue_baseline_nodes` already contains `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`; keep it there and map its `task` to S4-02d. S5-01's `I-site-overview-read` remains gated on this node and the separate F1 node. No baseline node moves.
+
+Out of scope: F1 late-reading sequence semantics (`I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`), new cohort or fuel model, new owner UI/copy, S5-01 implementation, real gateway source-time attestation, and a general estate scheduler for unrelated domains.

+# S4-02d — analytic and Finding worker schedule
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: false, provided this remains an operations/read-contract slice with no new owner-facing copy, panel, or visual state. If implementation changes any of those, reopen the flag and obtain the owner checkpoint. Fresh independent implementation review remains required.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials, prerequisite of S5-01
+Depends on: registry `requires` `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work` (resolved). `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` is a separate S5-01 prerequisite, not an input to this schedule.
+Branch when activated: `task/S4-02d-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-14
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+An environment-owned, bounded tick runs S3 `process-jobs`, then S4 `match-findings` for every active tenant/Site, then the existing S4-02c consequence drain. A late accepted reading reaches a revised FindingRevision and consequence without an operator invoking the two Leg B commands. Operators can see per-Site backlog, last success/failure, retry and stopped-worker conditions. S5-01 cannot mark a selected window CURRENT while relevant upstream work or an unobserved/stopped worker could leave it behind.
+
+Authority: `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md` R1, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`. This packet owns registry node `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule` (S). The separate F1 node owns run-1 sequence numbering and money limitation semantics; this packet does not change S3 cohort rules, Finding limits or price eligibility.
+
+Built anchors: `.ai/CODE_STATE.md` S3-01/S4-01/S4-02c; `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assess_dispatch.py` (`process-jobs`, `match-findings --tenant-id --site-id`); `analytics/assessment_worker.py` (reconcile, leased jobs and retry); `backend/assetops_backend/recompute_consequences.py` and `financials/recompute.py` (Site claim, attempt-derived retry); `backend/README-db.md` (one-minute external-timer runbook); `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` (Leg A/B proof). The current `process-jobs` CLI reconciles all assessed Sites and drains up to 1000 jobs; `match-findings` is a one-shot Site matcher; the consequence CLI serves only the configured development tenant and one named Site. Do not claim those CLIs already cover all active production Sites.
+
+## Schedule, state and S5 contract
+
+1. **Owned Site inventory and cadence.** Configure one deployment-owned timer (Windows Task Scheduler or systemd) at a documented cadence, initially one minute. A server-side orchestrator enumerates active imported tenant/Sites from the authoritative persisted Site registry on every tick, including newly activated Sites; use stable tenant/Site IDs, never a hard-coded demo list or gateway-supplied scope. Define and test the exact active predicate and exclusion of inactive/foreign Sites. Bound Sites per tick, S3 jobs per tick and consequence items per Site, with a durable cursor or fair ordering so a busy Site cannot starve another. The existing S3 worker's global reconcile must still run; expose a bounded drain limit rather than relying on the CLI's default 1000. S4 matching runs only after S3 work relevant to that Site has reached a completed basis; if S3 remains due, leased, failed or deferred, record it as pending and defer that Site's S4/currentness promotion. Match the Site even when no new S3 job exists if a selected assessment/configuration/model basis became dirty. Then drain that Site's consequence work after Finding commits. One tick may leave work pending; later ticks resume it.
+2. **Concurrency and recovery.** Use a database claim for the schedule/tick and per-Site processing, or prove an equivalent cross-host exclusion; a local timer's “do not start another instance” is insufficient. Preserve S3's lease/retry and S4-02c's Site claim/append idempotency. A crash releases or expires the claim, and the next tick resumes without duplicate assessment, FindingRevision or consequence basis. Bound wall time per tick and per Site, including a hung `match-findings`/database call; report timeout as failure and leave work retryable. Use a documented retry/backoff for schedule-level failures, including failed Site discovery, S3 worker exceptions and matcher exceptions; do not silently advance success. Do not add a second independent retry mechanism to S3 or financial work rows.
+3. **Observable durable state.** Persist or derive a tenant/Site-scoped schedule record with last tick start, last success, last failure (stage, typed code and time), running/claim age, retry due, selected/processed/deferred counts and remaining S3/S4/consequence backlog. Define S4 backlog by a durable dirty basis/watermark or equivalent exact comparison between selected assessment revisions and matched Finding basis, not by counting only queued S3 jobs. Expose a read-only operator report/CLI with Site identity and an aggregate; distinguish `RUNNING`, `BACKLOG`, `RETRY_DUE`, `FAILED`, `STOPPED` (missed heartbeat beyond a declared threshold), and healthy idle. A successful zero-work tick advances the heartbeat. Failed and repeatedly skipped/hung ticks cannot look healthy. Carry S4-02c review I1/I2: show permanently failing consequence attempt count/age and growing backlog without capping or deleting attempts; make repeated Site-claim skips and stale in-flight claims an alarm with a concrete recovery procedure. Document the timer identity, credentials, cadence, timeouts, alert threshold, restart and commands in `backend/README-db.md`.
+4. **S5-01 consumption.** Provide a scoped status/watermark read that S5-01 can join to its exact selected half-open Site window: accepted-source watermark, selected S3 run/revision, S4 matched revision/basis and outstanding/failed consequence work plus schedule heartbeat. A first build with outstanding work is `PENDING`; an older exact result with an advanced source/analytic basis or dirty work is `STALE_PROJECTION`; a failed worker with no usable prior result is `FAILED`, with the exact upstream stage/reason available. A stopped or unobserved schedule cannot certify CURRENT, even when a queue query is empty. Only a healthy observed pass after the accepted-source watermark, completed relevant S3 work, matched S4 basis and completed relevant consequence work may permit CURRENT; S5-01 still applies its separate F1 money rule and `CURRENT/PRICED` same-basis check. Keep old IDs and lineage readable. This packet exposes the contract and tests its gate; S5-01 implements the Overview read/UI.
+
+## Phases and focused proof
+
+Each phase is a reviewable result fitting one Claude Implementer context below approximately 120k tokens; obtain an independent fresh review before proceeding.
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Bounded scheduled orchestration | Persisted active-Site enumeration, fair bounded tick, ordered S3 → S4 → consequence invocation, cross-host claim, timeout/restart behavior and installable timer configuration. Prove two tenants, newly activated Site, inactive/foreign Site refusal, more Sites/jobs than one tick limit, two concurrent timers, crash/restart, idempotent replay and no S4 promotion ahead of due/leased/failed S3 work. |
+| 2. Health and currentness handoff | Durable/derived per-Site stage and backlog report, last success/failure, retry due and stopped/hung alarm; selected-window S5 status/watermark seam. Prove idle heartbeat, failing matcher, failed S3 job, repeated claim skips, timer stopped beyond threshold, permanent consequence failure and recovery. Assert pending/stale/failed versus eligible CURRENT after reload; no queue-empty shortcut. |
+| 3. Automatic late-power walkthrough | Extend or reuse `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` in an isolated database. Retain Leg A exact accepted ID, stale queued work, same-revision `POWER_NOT_IN_ASSESSMENT_INPUTS` and history. Release the withheld GOOD mean, then drive only the configured schedule/ticks (no manual `process-jobs` or `match-findings` Leg B calls). Assert the revised FindingRevision cites the late ID; its consequence work reaches the new CURRENT result and passes the bounded S5 eligibility gate only after all upstream work is complete. Record actual run/revision/work/attempt/consequence IDs, watermarks, backlog and heartbeat before/after, replay and stopped-worker state. If run-1 admission numbering triggers F1 ambiguity, assert no S5 amount and use the existing source-ordered variant for the positive amount path; do not fix F1 here. |
+
+Reviewer checks Site inventory and isolation, per-tick limits/fairness, ordering, claim/timeout/retry, restart idempotency, stage-specific errors, heartbeat and backlog truth, currentness under race, and the no-manual-Leg-B artifact. Run focused Postgres worker tests and the isolated walkthrough plus applicable architecture/workflow guards. `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` stays false only while the output remains operator/read-contract work; S5-01 retains the owner visual checkpoint.
+
+## Risks and carries
+
+- S3's existing global `reconcile_all()` can be expensive as Sites grow; measure a multi-Site tick and keep bounded scheduling truthful. Do not omit reconcile to meet a timer budget.
+- S4 matching currently scans a Site's selected reads. Large Sites may require a reviewed bounded cursor or dirty-window selection to meet the tick limit; do not mark a partially matched Site caught up.
+- S4-02c retries have no terminal cap: a deterministic failed row can append about 90 attempts/day. Keep the growing count and backlog observable and alertable; do not hide it by reporting only due work.
+- A hung consequence runner can hold a Site claim while later ticks report skipped. Treat repeated skips/claim age as a stopped-progress alarm, with operator recovery; do not count skipped as success.
+- Backdated configuration/model changes can dirty assessed windows without a fresh evidence job. The S3 reconcile and S4 basis comparison must detect them before CURRENT is possible.
+
+Registry comparison: `queue_baseline_nodes` already contains `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`; keep it there and map its `task` to S4-02d. S5-01's `I-site-overview-read` remains gated on this node and the separate F1 node. No baseline node moves.
+
+Out of scope: F1 late-reading sequence semantics (`I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`), new cohort or fuel model, new owner UI/copy, S5-01 implementation, real gateway source-time attestation, and a general estate scheduler for unrelated domains.

apply patch

+# S4-02d — analytic and Finding worker schedule
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: false, provided this remains an operations/read-contract slice with no new owner-facing copy, panel, or visual state. If implementation changes any of those, reopen the flag and obtain the owner checkpoint. Fresh independent implementation review remains required.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials, prerequisite of S5-01
+Depends on: registry `requires` `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work` (resolved). `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` is a separate S5-01 prerequisite, not an input to this schedule.
+Branch when activated: `task/S4-02d-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-14
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+An environment-owned, bounded tick runs S3 `process-jobs`, then S4 `match-findings` for every active tenant/Site, then the existing S4-02c consequence drain. A late accepted reading reaches a revised FindingRevision and consequence without an operator invoking the two Leg B commands. Operators can see per-Site backlog, last success/failure, retry and stopped-worker conditions. S5-01 cannot mark a selected window CURRENT while relevant upstream work or an unobserved/stopped worker could leave it behind.
+
+Authority: `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md` R1, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`. This packet owns registry node `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule` (S). The separate F1 node owns run-1 sequence numbering and money limitation semantics; this packet does not change S3 cohort rules, Finding limits or price eligibility.
+
+Built anchors: `.ai/CODE_STATE.md` S3-01/S4-01/S4-02c; `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assess_dispatch.py` (`process-jobs`, `match-findings --tenant-id --site-id`); `analytics/assessment_worker.py` (reconcile, leased jobs and retry); `backend/assetops_backend/recompute_consequences.py` and `financials/recompute.py` (Site claim, attempt-derived retry); `backend/README-db.md` (one-minute external-timer runbook); `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` (Leg A/B proof). The current `process-jobs` CLI reconciles all assessed Sites and drains up to 1000 jobs; `match-findings` is a one-shot Site matcher; the consequence CLI serves only the configured development tenant and one named Site. Do not claim those CLIs already cover all active production Sites.
+
+## Schedule, state and S5 contract
+
+1. **Owned Site inventory and cadence.** Configure one deployment-owned timer (Windows Task Scheduler or systemd) at a documented cadence, initially one minute. A server-side orchestrator enumerates active imported tenant/Sites from the authoritative persisted Site registry on every tick, including newly activated Sites; use stable tenant/Site IDs, never a hard-coded demo list or gateway-supplied scope. Define and test the exact active predicate and exclusion of inactive/foreign Sites. Bound Sites per tick, S3 jobs per tick and consequence items per Site, with a durable cursor or fair ordering so a busy Site cannot starve another. The existing S3 worker's global reconcile must still run; expose a bounded drain limit rather than relying on the CLI's default 1000. S4 matching runs only after S3 work relevant to that Site has reached a completed basis; if S3 remains due, leased, failed or deferred, record it as pending and defer that Site's S4/currentness promotion. Match the Site even when no new S3 job exists if a selected assessment/configuration/model basis became dirty. Then drain that Site's consequence work after Finding commits. One tick may leave work pending; later ticks resume it.
+2. **Concurrency and recovery.** Use a database claim for the schedule/tick and per-Site processing, or prove an equivalent cross-host exclusion; a local timer's “do not start another instance” is insufficient. Preserve S3's lease/retry and S4-02c's Site claim/append idempotency. A crash releases or expires the claim, and the next tick resumes without duplicate assessment, FindingRevision or consequence basis. Bound wall time per tick and per Site, including a hung `match-findings`/database call; report timeout as failure and leave work retryable. Use a documented retry/backoff for schedule-level failures, including failed Site discovery, S3 worker exceptions and matcher exceptions; do not silently advance success. Do not add a second independent retry mechanism to S3 or financial work rows.
+3. **Observable durable state.** Persist or derive a tenant/Site-scoped schedule record with last tick start, last success, last failure (stage, typed code and time), running/claim age, retry due, selected/processed/deferred counts and remaining S3/S4/consequence backlog. Define S4 backlog by a durable dirty basis/watermark or equivalent exact comparison between selected assessment revisions and matched Finding basis, not by counting only queued S3 jobs. Expose a read-only operator report/CLI with Site identity and an aggregate; distinguish `RUNNING`, `BACKLOG`, `RETRY_DUE`, `FAILED`, `STOPPED` (missed heartbeat beyond a declared threshold), and healthy idle. A successful zero-work tick advances the heartbeat. Failed and repeatedly skipped/hung ticks cannot look healthy. Carry S4-02c review I1/I2: show permanently failing consequence attempt count/age and growing backlog without capping or deleting attempts; make repeated Site-claim skips and stale in-flight claims an alarm with a concrete recovery procedure. Document the timer identity, credentials, cadence, timeouts, alert threshold, restart and commands in `backend/README-db.md`.
+4. **S5-01 consumption.** Provide a scoped status/watermark read that S5-01 can join to its exact selected half-open Site window: accepted-source watermark, selected S3 run/revision, S4 matched revision/basis and outstanding/failed consequence work plus schedule heartbeat. A first build with outstanding work is `PENDING`; an older exact result with an advanced source/analytic basis or dirty work is `STALE_PROJECTION`; a failed worker with no usable prior result is `FAILED`, with the exact upstream stage/reason available. A stopped or unobserved schedule cannot certify CURRENT, even when a queue query is empty. Only a healthy observed pass after the accepted-source watermark, completed relevant S3 work, matched S4 basis and completed relevant consequence work may permit CURRENT; S5-01 still applies its separate F1 money rule and `CURRENT/PRICED` same-basis check. Keep old IDs and lineage readable. This packet exposes the contract and tests its gate; S5-01 implements the Overview read/UI.
+
+## Phases and focused proof
+
+Each phase is a reviewable result fitting one Claude Implementer context below approximately 120k tokens; obtain an independent fresh review before proceeding.
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Bounded scheduled orchestration | Persisted active-Site enumeration, fair bounded tick, ordered S3 → S4 → consequence invocation, cross-host claim, timeout/restart behavior and installable timer configuration. Prove two tenants, newly activated Site, inactive/foreign Site refusal, more Sites/jobs than one tick limit, two concurrent timers, crash/restart, idempotent replay and no S4 promotion ahead of due/leased/failed S3 work. |
+| 2. Health and currentness handoff | Durable/derived per-Site stage and backlog report, last success/failure, retry due and stopped/hung alarm; selected-window S5 status/watermark seam. Prove idle heartbeat, failing matcher, failed S3 job, repeated claim skips, timer stopped beyond threshold, permanent consequence failure and recovery. Assert pending/stale/failed versus eligible CURRENT after reload; no queue-empty shortcut. |
+| 3. Automatic late-power walkthrough | Extend or reuse `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` in an isolated database. Retain Leg A exact accepted ID, stale queued work, same-revision `POWER_NOT_IN_ASSESSMENT_INPUTS` and history. Release the withheld GOOD mean, then drive only the configured schedule/ticks (no manual `process-jobs` or `match-findings` Leg B calls). Assert the revised FindingRevision cites the late ID; its consequence work reaches the new CURRENT result and passes the bounded S5 eligibility gate only after all upstream work is complete. Record actual run/revision/work/attempt/consequence IDs, watermarks, backlog and heartbeat before/after, replay and stopped-worker state. If run-1 admission numbering triggers F1 ambiguity, assert no S5 amount and use the existing source-ordered variant for the positive amount path; do not fix F1 here. |
+
+Reviewer checks Site inventory and isolation, per-tick limits/fairness, ordering, claim/timeout/retry, restart idempotency, stage-specific errors, heartbeat and backlog truth, currentness under race, and the no-manual-Leg-B artifact. Run focused Postgres worker tests and the isolated walkthrough plus applicable architecture/workflow guards. `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` stays false only while the output remains operator/read-contract work; S5-01 retains the owner visual checkpoint.
+
+## Risks and carries
+
+- S3's existing global `reconcile_all()` can be expensive as Sites grow; measure a multi-Site tick and keep bounded scheduling truthful. Do not omit reconcile to meet a timer budget.
+- S4 matching currently scans a Site's selected reads. Large Sites may require a reviewed bounded cursor or dirty-window selection to meet the tick limit; do not mark a partially matched Site caught up.
+- S4-02c retries have no terminal cap: a deterministic failed row can append about 90 attempts/day. Keep the growing count and backlog observable and alertable; do not hide it by reporting only due work.
+- A hung consequence runner can hold a Site claim while later ticks report skipped. Treat repeated skips/claim age as a stopped-progress alarm, with operator recovery; do not count skipped as success.
+- Backdated configuration/model changes can dirty assessed windows without a fresh evidence job. The S3 reconcile and S4 basis comparison must detect them before CURRENT is possible.
+
+Registry comparison: `queue_baseline_nodes` already contains `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`; keep it there and map its `task` to S4-02d. S5-01's `I-site-overview-read` remains gated on this node and the separate F1 node. No baseline node moves.
+
+Out of scope: F1 late-reading sequence semantics (`I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`), new cohort or fuel model, new owner UI/copy, S5-01 implementation, real gateway source-time attestation, and a general estate scheduler for unrelated domains.

+# S4-02d — analytic and Finding worker schedule
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: false, provided this remains an operations/read-contract slice with no new owner-facing copy, panel, or visual state. If implementation changes any of those, reopen the flag and obtain the owner checkpoint. Fresh independent implementation review remains required.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials, prerequisite of S5-01
+Depends on: registry `requires` `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work` (resolved). `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` is a separate S5-01 prerequisite, not an input to this schedule.
+Branch when activated: `task/S4-02d-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-14
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+An environment-owned, bounded tick runs S3 `process-jobs`, then S4 `match-findings` for every active tenant/Site, then the existing S4-02c consequence drain. A late accepted reading reaches a revised FindingRevision and consequence without an operator invoking the two Leg B commands. Operators can see per-Site backlog, last success/failure, retry and stopped-worker conditions. S5-01 cannot mark a selected window CURRENT while relevant upstream work or an unobserved/stopped worker could leave it behind.
+
+Authority: `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md` R1, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`. This packet owns registry node `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule` (S). The separate F1 node owns run-1 sequence numbering and money limitation semantics; this packet does not change S3 cohort rules, Finding limits or price eligibility.
+
+Built anchors: `.ai/CODE_STATE.md` S3-01/S4-01/S4-02c; `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assess_dispatch.py` (`process-jobs`, `match-findings --tenant-id --site-id`); `analytics/assessment_worker.py` (reconcile, leased jobs and retry); `backend/assetops_backend/recompute_consequences.py` and `financials/recompute.py` (Site claim, attempt-derived retry); `backend/README-db.md` (one-minute external-timer runbook); `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` (Leg A/B proof). The current `process-jobs` CLI reconciles all assessed Sites and drains up to 1000 jobs; `match-findings` is a one-shot Site matcher; the consequence CLI serves only the configured development tenant and one named Site. Do not claim those CLIs already cover all active production Sites.
+
+## Schedule, state and S5 contract
+
+1. **Owned Site inventory and cadence.** Configure one deployment-owned timer (Windows Task Scheduler or systemd) at a documented cadence, initially one minute. A server-side orchestrator enumerates active imported tenant/Sites from the authoritative persisted Site registry on every tick, including newly activated Sites; use stable tenant/Site IDs, never a hard-coded demo list or gateway-supplied scope. Define and test the exact active predicate and exclusion of inactive/foreign Sites. Bound Sites per tick, S3 jobs per tick and consequence items per Site, with a durable cursor or fair ordering so a busy Site cannot starve another. The existing S3 worker's global reconcile must still run; expose a bounded drain limit rather than relying on the CLI's default 1000. S4 matching runs only after S3 work relevant to that Site has reached a completed basis; if S3 remains due, leased, failed or deferred, record it as pending and defer that Site's S4/currentness promotion. Match the Site even when no new S3 job exists if a selected assessment/configuration/model basis became dirty. Then drain that Site's consequence work after Finding commits. One tick may leave work pending; later ticks resume it.
+2. **Concurrency and recovery.** Use a database claim for the schedule/tick and per-Site processing, or prove an equivalent cross-host exclusion; a local timer's “do not start another instance” is insufficient. Preserve S3's lease/retry and S4-02c's Site claim/append idempotency. A crash releases or expires the claim, and the next tick resumes without duplicate assessment, FindingRevision or consequence basis. Bound wall time per tick and per Site, including a hung `match-findings`/database call; report timeout as failure and leave work retryable. Use a documented retry/backoff for schedule-level failures, including failed Site discovery, S3 worker exceptions and matcher exceptions; do not silently advance success. Do not add a second independent retry mechanism to S3 or financial work rows.
+3. **Observable durable state.** Persist or derive a tenant/Site-scoped schedule record with last tick start, last success, last failure (stage, typed code and time), running/claim age, retry due, selected/processed/deferred counts and remaining S3/S4/consequence backlog. Define S4 backlog by a durable dirty basis/watermark or equivalent exact comparison between selected assessment revisions and matched Finding basis, not by counting only queued S3 jobs. Expose a read-only operator report/CLI with Site identity and an aggregate; distinguish `RUNNING`, `BACKLOG`, `RETRY_DUE`, `FAILED`, `STOPPED` (missed heartbeat beyond a declared threshold), and healthy idle. A successful zero-work tick advances the heartbeat. Failed and repeatedly skipped/hung ticks cannot look healthy. Carry S4-02c review I1/I2: show permanently failing consequence attempt count/age and growing backlog without capping or deleting attempts; make repeated Site-claim skips and stale in-flight claims an alarm with a concrete recovery procedure. Document the timer identity, credentials, cadence, timeouts, alert threshold, restart and commands in `backend/README-db.md`.
+4. **S5-01 consumption.** Provide a scoped status/watermark read that S5-01 can join to its exact selected half-open Site window: accepted-source watermark, selected S3 run/revision, S4 matched revision/basis and outstanding/failed consequence work plus schedule heartbeat. A first build with outstanding work is `PENDING`; an older exact result with an advanced source/analytic basis or dirty work is `STALE_PROJECTION`; a failed worker with no usable prior result is `FAILED`, with the exact upstream stage/reason available. A stopped or unobserved schedule cannot certify CURRENT, even when a queue query is empty. Only a healthy observed pass after the accepted-source watermark, completed relevant S3 work, matched S4 basis and completed relevant consequence work may permit CURRENT; S5-01 still applies its separate F1 money rule and `CURRENT/PRICED` same-basis check. Keep old IDs and lineage readable. This packet exposes the contract and tests its gate; S5-01 implements the Overview read/UI.
+
+## Phases and focused proof
+
+Each phase is a reviewable result fitting one Claude Implementer context below approximately 120k tokens; obtain an independent fresh review before proceeding.
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Bounded scheduled orchestration | Persisted active-Site enumeration, fair bounded tick, ordered S3 → S4 → consequence invocation, cross-host claim, timeout/restart behavior and installable timer configuration. Prove two tenants, newly activated Site, inactive/foreign Site refusal, more Sites/jobs than one tick limit, two concurrent timers, crash/restart, idempotent replay and no S4 promotion ahead of due/leased/failed S3 work. |
+| 2. Health and currentness handoff | Durable/derived per-Site stage and backlog report, last success/failure, retry due and stopped/hung alarm; selected-window S5 status/watermark seam. Prove idle heartbeat, failing matcher, failed S3 job, repeated claim skips, timer stopped beyond threshold, permanent consequence failure and recovery. Assert pending/stale/failed versus eligible CURRENT after reload; no queue-empty shortcut. |
+| 3. Automatic late-power walkthrough | Extend or reuse `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` in an isolated database. Retain Leg A exact accepted ID, stale queued work, same-revision `POWER_NOT_IN_ASSESSMENT_INPUTS` and history. Release the withheld GOOD mean, then drive only the configured schedule/ticks (no manual `process-jobs` or `match-findings` Leg B calls). Assert the revised FindingRevision cites the late ID; its consequence work reaches the new CURRENT result and passes the bounded S5 eligibility gate only after all upstream work is complete. Record actual run/revision/work/attempt/consequence IDs, watermarks, backlog and heartbeat before/after, replay and stopped-worker state. If run-1 admission numbering triggers F1 ambiguity, assert no S5 amount and use the existing source-ordered variant for the positive amount path; do not fix F1 here. |
+
+Reviewer checks Site inventory and isolation, per-tick limits/fairness, ordering, claim/timeout/retry, restart idempotency, stage-specific errors, heartbeat and backlog truth, currentness under race, and the no-manual-Leg-B artifact. Run focused Postgres worker tests and the isolated walkthrough plus applicable architecture/workflow guards. `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` stays false only while the output remains operator/read-contract work; S5-01 retains the owner visual checkpoint.
+
+## Risks and carries
+
+- S3's existing global `reconcile_all()` can be expensive as Sites grow; measure a multi-Site tick and keep bounded scheduling truthful. Do not omit reconcile to meet a timer budget.
+- S4 matching currently scans a Site's selected reads. Large Sites may require a reviewed bounded cursor or dirty-window selection to meet the tick limit; do not mark a partially matched Site caught up.
+- S4-02c retries have no terminal cap: a deterministic failed row can append about 90 attempts/day. Keep the growing count and backlog observable and alertable; do not hide it by reporting only due work.
+- A hung consequence runner can hold a Site claim while later ticks report skipped. Treat repeated skips/claim age as a stopped-progress alarm, with operator recovery; do not count skipped as success.
+- Backdated configuration/model changes can dirty assessed windows without a fresh evidence job. The S3 reconcile and S4 basis comparison must detect them before CURRENT is possible.
+
+Registry comparison: `queue_baseline_nodes` already contains `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`; keep it there and map its `task` to S4-02d. S5-01's `I-site-overview-read` remains gated on this node and the separate F1 node. No baseline node moves.
+
+Out of scope: F1 late-reading sequence semantics (`I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`), new cohort or fuel model, new owner UI/copy, S5-01 implementation, real gateway source-time attestation, and a general estate scheduler for unrelated domains.

+# S4-02d — analytic and Finding worker schedule
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: false, provided this remains an operations/read-contract slice with no new owner-facing copy, panel, or visual state. If implementation changes any of those, reopen the flag and obtain the owner checkpoint. Fresh independent implementation review remains required.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials, prerequisite of S5-01
+Depends on: registry `requires` `I-S3-01`, `I-S4-01`, `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work` (resolved). `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` is a separate S5-01 prerequisite, not an input to this schedule.
+Branch when activated: `task/S4-02d-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-14
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+An environment-owned, bounded tick runs S3 `process-jobs`, then S4 `match-findings` for every active tenant/Site, then the existing S4-02c consequence drain. A late accepted reading reaches a revised FindingRevision and consequence without an operator invoking the two Leg B commands. Operators can see per-Site backlog, last success/failure, retry and stopped-worker conditions. S5-01 cannot mark a selected window CURRENT while relevant upstream work or an unobserved/stopped worker could leave it behind.
+
+Authority: `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md` R1, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`. This packet owns registry node `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule` (S). The separate F1 node owns run-1 sequence numbering and money limitation semantics; this packet does not change S3 cohort rules, Finding limits or price eligibility.
+
+Built anchors: `.ai/CODE_STATE.md` S3-01/S4-01/S4-02c; `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assess_dispatch.py` (`process-jobs`, `match-findings --tenant-id --site-id`); `analytics/assessment_worker.py` (reconcile, leased jobs and retry); `backend/assetops_backend/recompute_consequences.py` and `financials/recompute.py` (Site claim, attempt-derived retry); `backend/README-db.md` (one-minute external-timer runbook); `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` (Leg A/B proof). The current `process-jobs` CLI reconciles all assessed Sites and drains up to 1000 jobs; `match-findings` is a one-shot Site matcher; the consequence CLI serves only the configured development tenant and one named Site. Do not claim those CLIs already cover all active production Sites.
+
+## Schedule, state and S5 contract
+
+1. **Owned Site inventory and cadence.** Configure one deployment-owned timer (Windows Task Scheduler or systemd) at a documented cadence, initially one minute. A server-side orchestrator enumerates active imported tenant/Sites from the authoritative persisted Site registry on every tick, including newly activated Sites; use stable tenant/Site IDs, never a hard-coded demo list or gateway-supplied scope. Define and test the exact active predicate and exclusion of inactive/foreign Sites. Bound Sites per tick, S3 jobs per tick and consequence items per Site, with a durable cursor or fair ordering so a busy Site cannot starve another. The existing S3 worker's global reconcile must still run; expose a bounded drain limit rather than relying on the CLI's default 1000. S4 matching runs only after S3 work relevant to that Site has reached a completed basis; if S3 remains due, leased, failed or deferred, record it as pending and defer that Site's S4/currentness promotion. Match the Site even when no new S3 job exists if a selected assessment/configuration/model basis became dirty. Then drain that Site's consequence work after Finding commits. One tick may leave work pending; later ticks resume it.
+2. **Concurrency and recovery.** Use a database claim for the schedule/tick and per-Site processing, or prove an equivalent cross-host exclusion; a local timer's “do not start another instance” is insufficient. Preserve S3's lease/retry and S4-02c's Site claim/append idempotency. A crash releases or expires the claim, and the next tick resumes without duplicate assessment, FindingRevision or consequence basis. Bound wall time per tick and per Site, including a hung `match-findings`/database call; report timeout as failure and leave work retryable. Use a documented retry/backoff for schedule-level failures, including failed Site discovery, S3 worker exceptions and matcher exceptions; do not silently advance success. Do not add a second independent retry mechanism to S3 or financial work rows.
+3. **Observable durable state.** Persist or derive a tenant/Site-scoped schedule record with last tick start, last success, last failure (stage, typed code and time), running/claim age, retry due, selected/processed/deferred counts and remaining S3/S4/consequence backlog. Define S4 backlog by a durable dirty basis/watermark or equivalent exact comparison between selected assessment revisions and matched Finding basis, not by counting only queued S3 jobs. Expose a read-only operator report/CLI with Site identity and an aggregate; distinguish `RUNNING`, `BACKLOG`, `RETRY_DUE`, `FAILED`, `STOPPED` (missed heartbeat beyond a declared threshold), and healthy idle. A successful zero-work tick advances the heartbeat. Failed and repeatedly skipped/hung ticks cannot look healthy. Carry S4-02c review I1/I2: show permanently failing consequence attempt count/age and growing backlog without capping or deleting attempts; make repeated Site-claim skips and stale in-flight claims an alarm with a concrete recovery procedure. Document the timer identity, credentials, cadence, timeouts, alert threshold, restart and commands in `backend/README-db.md`.
+4. **S5-01 consumption.** Provide a scoped status/watermark read that S5-01 can join to its exact selected half-open Site window: accepted-source watermark, selected S3 run/revision, S4 matched revision/basis and outstanding/failed consequence work plus schedule heartbeat. A first build with outstanding work is `PENDING`; an older exact result with an advanced source/analytic basis or dirty work is `STALE_PROJECTION`; a failed worker with no usable prior result is `FAILED`, with the exact upstream stage/reason available. A stopped or unobserved schedule cannot certify CURRENT, even when a queue query is empty. Only a healthy observed pass after the accepted-source watermark, completed relevant S3 work, matched S4 basis and completed relevant consequence work may permit CURRENT; S5-01 still applies its separate F1 money rule and `CURRENT/PRICED` same-basis check. Keep old IDs and lineage readable. This packet exposes the contract and tests its gate; S5-01 implements the Overview read/UI.
+
+## Phases and focused proof
+
+Each phase is a reviewable result fitting one Claude Implementer context below approximately 120k tokens; obtain an independent fresh review before proceeding.
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Bounded scheduled orchestration | Persisted active-Site enumeration, fair bounded tick, ordered S3 → S4 → consequence invocation, cross-host claim, timeout/restart behavior and installable timer configuration. Prove two tenants, newly activated Site, inactive/foreign Site refusal, more Sites/jobs than one tick limit, two concurrent timers, crash/restart, idempotent replay and no S4 promotion ahead of due/leased/failed S3 work. |
+| 2. Health and currentness handoff | Durable/derived per-Site stage and backlog report, last success/failure, retry due and stopped/hung alarm; selected-window S5 status/watermark seam. Prove idle heartbeat, failing matcher, failed S3 job, repeated claim skips, timer stopped beyond threshold, permanent consequence failure and recovery. Assert pending/stale/failed versus eligible CURRENT after reload; no queue-empty shortcut. |
+| 3. Automatic late-power walkthrough | Extend or reuse `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` in an isolated database. Retain Leg A exact accepted ID, stale queued work, same-revision `POWER_NOT_IN_ASSESSMENT_INPUTS` and history. Release the withheld GOOD mean, then drive only the configured schedule/ticks (no manual `process-jobs` or `match-findings` Leg B calls). Assert the revised FindingRevision cites the late ID; its consequence work reaches the new CURRENT result and passes the bounded S5 eligibility gate only after all upstream work is complete. Record actual run/revision/work/attempt/consequence IDs, watermarks, backlog and heartbeat before/after, replay and stopped-worker state. If run-1 admission numbering triggers F1 ambiguity, assert no S5 amount and use the existing source-ordered variant for the positive amount path; do not fix F1 here. |
+
+Reviewer checks Site inventory and isolation, per-tick limits/fairness, ordering, claim/timeout/retry, restart idempotency, stage-specific errors, heartbeat and backlog truth, currentness under race, and the no-manual-Leg-B artifact. Run focused Postgres worker tests and the isolated walkthrough plus applicable architecture/workflow guards. `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` stays false only while the output remains operator/read-contract work; S5-01 retains the owner visual checkpoint.
+
+## Risks and carries
+
+- S3's existing global `reconcile_all()` can be expensive as Sites grow; measure a multi-Site tick and keep bounded scheduling truthful. Do not omit reconcile to meet a timer budget.
+- S4 matching currently scans a Site's selected reads. Large Sites may require a reviewed bounded cursor or dirty-window selection to meet the tick limit; do not mark a partially matched Site caught up.
+- S4-02c retries have no terminal cap: a deterministic failed row can append about 90 attempts/day. Keep the growing count and backlog observable and alertable; do not hide it by reporting only due work.
+- A hung consequence runner can hold a Site claim while later ticks report skipped. Treat repeated skips/claim age as a stopped-progress alarm, with operator recovery; do not count skipped as success.
+- Backdated configuration/model changes can dirty assessed windows without a fresh evidence job. The S3 reconcile and S4 basis comparison must detect them before CURRENT is possible.
+
+Registry comparison: `queue_baseline_nodes` already contains `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`; keep it there and map its `task` to S4-02d. S5-01's `I-site-overview-read` remains gated on this node and the separate F1 node. No baseline node moves.
+
+Out of scope: F1 late-reading sequence semantics (`I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`), new cohort or fuel model, new owner UI/copy, S5-01 implementation, real gateway source-time attestation, and a general estate scheduler for unrelated domains.

+Authority: `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md` R1, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`. This packet owns registry node `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule` (S). The separate F1 node owns run-1 sequence numbering and money limitation semantics; this packet does not change S3 cohort rules, Finding limits or price eligibility.

+Built anchors: `.ai/CODE_STATE.md` S3-01/S4-01/S4-02c; `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assess_dispatch.py` (`process-jobs`, `match-findings --tenant-id --site-id`); `analytics/assessment_worker.py` (reconcile, leased jobs and retry); `backend/assetops_backend/recompute_consequences.py` and `financials/recompute.py` (Site claim, attempt-derived retry); `backend/README-db.md` (one-minute external-timer runbook); `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` (Leg A/B proof). The current `process-jobs` CLI reconciles all assessed Sites and drains up to 1000 jobs; `match-findings` is a one-shot Site matcher; the consequence CLI serves only the configured development tenant and one named Site. Do not claim those CLIs already cover all active production Sites.
+
+## Schedule, state and S5 contract
+
+1. **Owned Site inventory and cadence.** Configure one deployment-owned timer (Windows Task Scheduler or systemd) at a documented cadence, initially one minute. A server-side orchestrator enumerates active imported tenant/Sites from the authoritative persisted Site registry on every tick, including newly activated Sites; use stable tenant/Site IDs, never a hard-coded demo list or gateway-supplied scope. Define and test the exact active predicate and exclusion of inactive/foreign Sites. Bound Sites per tick, S3 jobs per tick and consequence items per Site, with a durable cursor or fair ordering so a busy Site cannot starve another. The existing S3 worker's global reconcile must still run; expose a bounded drain limit rather than relying on the CLI's default 1000. S4 matching runs only after S3 work relevant to that Site has reached a completed basis; if S3 remains due, leased, failed or deferred, record it as pending and defer that Site's S4/currentness promotion. Match the Site even when no new S3 job exists if a selected assessment/configuration/model basis became dirty. Then drain that Site's consequence work after Finding commits. One tick may leave work pending; later ticks resume it.
+2. **Concurrency and recovery.** Use a database claim for the schedule/tick and per-Site processing, or prove an equivalent cross-host exclusion; a local timer's “do not start another instance” is insufficient. Preserve S3's lease/retry and S4-02c's Site claim/append idempotency. A crash releases or expires the claim, and the next tick resumes without duplicate assessment, FindingRevision or consequence basis. Bound wall time per tick and per Site, including a hung `match-findings`/database call; report timeout as failure and leave work retryable. Use a documented retry/backoff for schedule-level failures, including failed Site discovery, S3 worker exceptions and matcher exceptions; do not silently advance success. Do not add a second independent retry mechanism to S3 or financial work rows.
+3. **Observable durable state.** Persist or derive a tenant/Site-scoped schedule record with last tick start, last success, last failure (stage, typed code and time), running/claim age, retry due, selected/processed/deferred counts and remaining S3/S4/consequence backlog. Define S4 backlog by a durable dirty basis/watermark or equivalent exact comparison between selected assessment revisions and matched Finding basis, not by counting only queued S3 jobs. Expose a read-only operator report/CLI with Site identity and an aggregate; distinguish `RUNNING`, `BACKLOG`, `RETRY_DUE`, `FAILED`, `STOPPED` (missed heartbeat beyond a declared threshold), and healthy idle. A successful zero-work tick advances the heartbeat. Failed and repeatedly skipped/hung ticks cannot look healthy. Carry S4-02c review I1/I2: show permanently failing consequence attempt count/age and growing backlog without capping or deleting attempts; make repeated Site-claim skips and stale in-flight claims an alarm with a concrete recovery procedure. Document the timer identity, credentials, cadence, timeouts, alert threshold, restart and commands in `backend/README-db.md`.
+4. **S5-01 consumption.** Provide a scoped status/watermark read that S5-01 can join to its exact selected half-open Site window: accepted-source watermark, selected S3 run/revision, S4 matched revision/basis and outstanding/failed consequence work plus schedule heartbeat. A first build with outstanding work is `PENDING`; an older exact result with an advanced source/analytic basis or dirty work is `STALE_PROJECTION`; a failed worker with no usable prior result is `FAILED`, with the exact upstream stage/reason available. A stopped or unobserved schedule cannot certify CURRENT, even when a queue query is empty. Only a healthy observed pass after the accepted-source watermark, completed relevant S3 work, matched S4 basis and completed relevant consequence work may permit CURRENT; S5-01 still applies its separate F1 money rule and `CURRENT/PRICED` same-basis check. Keep old IDs and lineage readable. This packet exposes the contract and tests its gate; S5-01 implements the Overview read/UI.
+
+## Phases and focused proof
+
+Each phase is a reviewable result fitting one Claude Implementer context below approximately 120k tokens; obtain an independent fresh review before proceeding.
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Bounded scheduled orchestration | Persisted active-Site enumeration, fair bounded tick, ordered S3 → S4 → consequence invocation, cross-host claim, timeout/restart behavior and installable timer configuration. Prove two tenants, newly activated Site, inactive/foreign Site refusal, more Sites/jobs than one tick limit, two concurrent timers, crash/restart, idempotent replay and no S4 promotion ahead of due/leased/failed S3 work. |
+| 2. Health and currentness handoff | Durable/derived per-Site stage and backlog report, last success/failure, retry due and stopped/hung alarm; selected-window S5 status/watermark seam. Prove idle heartbeat, failing matcher, failed S3 job, repeated claim skips, timer stopped beyond threshold, permanent consequence failure and recovery. Assert pending/stale/failed versus eligible CURRENT after reload; no queue-empty shortcut. |
+| 3. Automatic late-power walkthrough | Extend or reuse `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` in an isolated database. Retain Leg A exact accepted ID, stale queued work, same-revision `POWER_NOT_IN_ASSESSMENT_INPUTS` and history. Release the withheld GOOD mean, then drive only the configured schedule/ticks (no manual `process-jobs` or `match-findings` Leg B calls). Assert the revised FindingRevision cites the late ID; its consequence work reaches the new CURRENT result and passes the bounded S5 eligibility gate only after all upstream work is complete. Record actual run/revision/work/attempt/consequence IDs, watermarks, backlog and heartbeat before/after, replay and stopped-worker state. If run-1 admission numbering triggers F1 ambiguity, assert no S5 amount and use the existing source-ordered variant for the positive amount path; do not fix F1 here. |
+
+Reviewer checks Site inventory and isolation, per-tick limits/fairness, ordering, claim/timeout/retry, restart idempotency, stage-specific errors, heartbeat and backlog truth, currentness under race, and the no-manual-Leg-B artifact. Run focused Postgres worker tests and the isolated walkthrough plus applicable architecture/workflow guards. `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` stays false only while the output remains operator/read-contract work; S5-01 retains the owner visual checkpoint.
+
+## Risks and carries
+
+- S3's existing global `reconcile_all()` can be expensive as Sites grow; measure a multi-Site tick and keep bounded scheduling truthful. Do not omit reconcile to meet a timer budget.
+- S4 matching currently scans a Site's selected reads. Large Sites may require a reviewed bounded cursor or dirty-window selection to meet the tick limit; do not mark a partially matched Site caught up.
+- S4-02c retries have no terminal cap: a deterministic failed row can append about 90 attempts/day. Keep the growing count and backlog observable and alertable; do not hide it by reporting only due work.
+- A hung consequence runner can hold a Site claim while later ticks report skipped. Treat repeated skips/claim age as a stopped-progress alarm, with operator recovery; do not count skipped as success.
+- Backdated configuration/model changes can dirty assessed windows without a fresh evidence job. The S3 reconcile and S4 basis comparison must detect them before CURRENT is possible.
+
+Registry comparison: `queue_baseline_nodes` already contains `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule`; keep it there and map its `task` to S4-02d. S5-01's `I-site-overview-read` remains gated on this node and the separate F1 node. No baseline node moves.
+
+Out of scope: F1 late-reading sequence semantics (`I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit`), new cohort or fuel model, new owner UI/copy, S5-01 implementation, real gateway source-time attestation, and a general estate scheduler for unrelated domains.
